TL;DR: 研究评估了地理空间基础模型AlphaEarth的嵌入向量是否能支持缅因州USA的二分类农田与非农田制图任务。在不微调基础模型的情况下，轻量级分类器达到93.7%总体准确率和90.8%平衡准确率，且Logistic回归与梯度提升集成方法表现相近。60,000个平衡像素样本与860万像素池结果相差仅1.3个百分点。在与人工判读对比中，AlphaEarth+随机森林方法在95.3%的点上与两位解释员共识一致，优于CDL的91.7%。结果表明冻结的地理空间嵌入是区域农田制图的低计算成本候选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是评估地理空间基础模型（如AlphaEarth）提供的可复用卫星影像表示是否能支持下游农田制图任务，特别是在标注数据有限的情况下。这种方法可以避免为每个新任务从头训练模型的高计算成本。

Method: 研究方法包括：1）使用192个空间分离的斑块进行评估，标签来源于USDA Cropland Data Layer (CDL)；2）在不微调基础模型的情况下，使用轻量级分类器（包括Logistic回归、梯度提升集成、最近类中心规则）进行二分类；3）在60,000个平衡像素样本与860万像素池中进行对比；4）进行2018-2023年的跨年转移实验；5）在385个随机采样点上与两位人工解释员的共识进行blind对比。

Result: 主要结果：1）轻量级分类器达到93.7%总体准确率和90.8%平衡准确率；2）Logistic回归与梯度提升集成差距在0.3个百分点内；3）最近类中心规则达到90.2%；4）60,000像素样本与全量860万像素结果相差1.3个百分点；5）跨年分类器在2018-2023年保持准确；6）与人工判读对比中，AlphaEarth+随机森林达到95.3%（κ=0.82），优于CDL的91.7%（κ=0.72），McNemar检验p=0.0161；7）与微调TerraMind模型差异不显著（95.3% vs 93.5%，p=0.14）。

Conclusion: 研究结论：冻结的地理空间嵌入是区域农田制图的低计算成本有效方案。但研究存在局限性：仅基于单一州（缅因州）研究、基于30米分辨率训练参考数据、以及单一连续区块的人工验证。实验结果与CDL标签噪声平滑一致，但未确立对全州参考产品的修正。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models provide reusable representations of satellite imagery that support downstream mapping with limited task-specific modelling. We evaluate whether annual AlphaEarth embeddings support binary cultivated-versus-non-cultivated mapping in Maine, USA, using 192 spatially separated patches and labels derived from the USDA Cropland Data Layer (CDL). Without fine-tuning the foundation model, a lightweight classifier reaches 93.7% overall accuracy and 90.8% balanced accuracy on held-out patches. Logistic regression is within 0.3 percentage points of a gradient-boosted ensemble, while a nearest-class-centroid rule, which uses class centroids but fits no parameters, reaches 90.2%. A balanced sample of 60,000 labelled pixels is within 1.3 percentage points of the full pool of 8.6 million pixels; because pixels are spatially autocorrelated, this result concerns pixel-sample efficiency rather than 60,000 independent annotation sites. In a same-region transfer experiment, classifiers trained in one year remain accurate across 2018 to 2023. Against a blind, two-interpreter consensus at 385 randomly sampled points in one contiguous 2023 block, the AlphaEarth-plus-random-forest map agrees at 95.3% ($κ=0.82$), compared with 91.7% for the CDL ($κ=0.72$; exact two-sided McNemar $p=0.0161$). This local result is consistent with partial smoothing of CDL label noise, but it does not establish statewide correction of the reference product. On the same points, the difference from a fine-tuned TerraMind segmentation model is not statistically significant (95.3% versus 93.5%; $p=0.14$), and the experiment is not a controlled comparison of computational cost. These results support frozen geospatial embeddings as a low-compute candidate for regional cropland mapping, subject to the limits of a single-state study, a 30 m-derived training reference, and a one-block human validation.

</details>


### [74] [ResLRP: The Role of Residual Cancellation in Attribution Instability in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17152)
*Jim Berend,Reduan Achtibat,Daniel Schäffer,Alexander Binder,Wojciech Samek,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Maximilian Dreyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ResLRP方法，解决Vision Transformers中Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP)产生的噪声多、不忠诚的归因问题。核心发现是残差连接中的取消效应导致归因爆炸，且在ViT中比语言Transformer更严重。ResLRP通过显式处理残差分支中的取消效应，提供精确保守且有界保证的归因传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers (ViTs)是现代视觉模型的核心，但获取细粒度、忠诚且稳定的输入归因仍然困难。LRP虽已适配到Transformer注意力机制，但在ViT中常产生噪声大、不忠诚的解释。

Method: 提出ResLRP (Residual-aware Layer-wise Relevance Propagation)，是LRP的简单扩展，其传播规则显式考虑残差分支中的取消效应，具有精确保守性并可证明地约束归因爆炸。论文通过因果通道干预确认是残差取消而非正则化效应导致不稳定性。

Result: ResLRP在多种ViT架构上显著提升归因质量，包括监督、自监督、对比、层次化和多模态家族，以及ground-truth-controlled的FunnyBirds基准。最大收益来自现代视觉语言模型(VLM)，实现+27-29%定位精度和高达3.4倍的忠诚度分数。此外还能定位Sparse Autoencoder特征，残差放大度量可作为预测归因退化的架构级诊断工具。

Conclusion: 残差连接的取消效应是ViT归因不稳定的关键原因，ResLRP通过显式建模残差路径成功解决了这一问题，在多种架构和基准上展现出显著改进，特别是在VLM中提升最大，表明该方法具有广泛的适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are central to most modern vision models, yet obtaining input attributions that are fine-grained, faithful, and stable remains challenging. Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) has been adapted to transformer attention, but in ViTs it often produces noisy, unfaithful explanations. We show that the missing ingredient is the treatment of residual connections: cancellation effects in residual pathways lead to attribution explosion. Moreover, we find that these cancellations are substantially stronger in ViTs than in language transformers. To address this issue, we introduce Residual-aware Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (ResLRP), a simple extension of LRP whose propagation rules explicitly account for cancellations in residual branches, are exactly conservative, and provably bound relevance explosion. Causal channel-wise interventions confirm that residual cancellation, not a generic regularization effect, drives the instability. ResLRP substantially improves attribution quality across faithfulness and localization, evaluated on ViT architectures spanning supervised, self-supervised, contrastive, hierarchical, and multimodal families, as well as on the ground-truth-controlled FunnyBirds benchmark. The largest gains arise in modern Vision Language Models (VLMs), with +27-29% localization and up to 3.4x faithfulness scores. Beyond benchmarks, ResLRP localizes Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) features in input space, and our residual amplification measure serves as an architecture-level diagnostic predicting where attribution degrades.

</details>


### [75] [MUMINS: Metadata-conditioned Uncertainty-aware Medical Image Next-state Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17169)
*Anna Oliveras,Roger Marí,Rafael Redondo,Oriol Guardià,Cynthia Ifeyinwa Ugwu,Ana Tost,Bhalaji Nagarajan,Carolina Migliorelli,Vicent Ribas,Petia Radeva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MUMINS是一个高效的医学图像扩散框架，用于预测随访扫描图像，可同时生成空间不确定性图。该方法在单次反向扩散过程中联合扩散基线扫描和随访残差，并通过负对数似然头学习不确定性图以标记错误风险区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在以下问题：1)确定性网络忽略生物随机性；2)标准扩散模型需要计算成本高的多次采样来量化不确定性。形态学变化相对于静态解剖结构变化微妙、高度患者特异性且本质上是随机的。

Method: 提出MUMINS框架，在单次反向扩散过程中联合扩散基线扫描及其随访残差（求和得到随访扫描），同时预测空间不确定性图。时间间隔和相关元数据作为条件，通过在每个去噪步骤动态重新注入基线作为软锚点来保留细粒度解剖结构，负对数似然头学习不确定性图。

Result: 在肺CT(PNG)和脑MRI(OASIS-3)数据集上，通过数据集特定重新训练，MUMINS与或优于专门的领域最先进方法。

Conclusion: MUMINS无需器官特定启发式方法，相同架构可通过数据集特定重新训练应用于不同解剖结构，在肺CT和脑MRI上展现出与或超越专用领域方法的性能。

Abstract: Forecasting anatomical changes such as tumor growth and neurodegeneration is a challenging generative vision task. Morphological evolution is subtle relative to static anatomy, highly patient-specific, and inherently stochastic. Existing methods struggle with several issues: deterministic networks ignore biological stochasticity, while standard diffusion models require computationally prohibitive multi-pass sampling to quantify uncertainty. We propose MUMINS (Metadata-conditioned Uncertainty-aware Medical Image Next-state Synthesis), an efficient diffusion framework that jointly diffuses a baseline scan and its follow-up residual, summed to synthesize the follow-up scan, while concurrently predicting a spatial uncertainty map, in a single reverse diffusion process. Conditioned on the time interval and relevant metadata, it preserves fine-grained anatomy by dynamically re-injecting the baseline as a soft anchor at every denoising step, and a negative-log-likelihood head learns the uncertainty map to explicitly flag error-prone regions. Designed without organ-specific heuristics, the same architecture is reused across anatomies via separate, dataset-specific retraining. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that dataset-specific retraining of MUMINS matches or outperforms dedicated, domain-specific state-of-the-art methods on lung CT (PNG) and brain MRI (OASIS-3). Project page: https://github.com/aolivtous/MUMINS.

</details>


### [76] [Multimodal Cultural Heritage Architectural Style Classification for Residential Buildings in the UAE Based on CLIP Embeddings and SVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17181)
*Ahmed Ammar Kubba,Manar Abu Talib,Iman Ibrahim,Qassim Nasir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态机器学习框架，利用OpenAI的CLIP模型对阿联酋住宅建筑风格进行分类。通过整合图像的视觉特征和专家描述的文本特征为统一的512维嵌入向量，结合UMAP降维和K-Means聚类，最终使用SVM分类器达到98%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于CNN的建筑风格分类方法过度依赖视觉图像，缺乏非西方区域特定数据集，且未有效利用文本描述信息。

Method: 使用CLIP模型将视觉和文本特征融合为512维嵌入向量，通过UMAP进行降维可视化，用K-Means进行无监督聚类，最后用SVM训练分类器。

Result: 在8个风格聚类上达到98%的分类准确率，高于文献中其他所有研究。

Conclusion: 多模态AI方法可有效支持建筑遗产分析，为探索区域建筑特征提供可扩展且可解释的工具。

Abstract: The analysis and classification of cultural heritage architectural styles remain challenging due to the complexity of visual images of buildings, which are highly relied on in traditional CNN-based classification approaches in comparison to textual descriptions, and the relative lack of non-western region-specific datasets. This paper addresses this gap by proposing a multimodal machine learning framework to analyze and classify Emirati residential architecture using OpenAI's CLIP model. We integrate visual features from images and textual features from expert descriptions into a unified 512-dimensional embedding, followed by dimensionality reduction with UMAP for visualization and unsupervised clustering using K-Means. Cluster labels, which are derived from manual analysis of the K-Means clusters, are used to train an SVM classifier for automated architectural style classification. Our approach achieves a classification accuracy of 98% across eight identified style clusters, higher than every other study in the literature, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining visual and textual modalities. Overall, this paper highlights the potential of using multimodal AI to support architectural heritage analysis, offering scalable and interpretable tools for exploring regional architectural identities.

</details>


### [77] [EventEgoHands++: Event-based Egocentric 3D Hand Mesh Reconstruction with Real Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17189)
*Ryosei Hara,Wataru Ikeda,Masashi Hatano,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EventEgoHands++是一个基于事件相机的自我中心视角3D手部网格重建框架，通过引入手部检测器和自适应注意力机制解决了以往方法无法区分左右手的问题，并在新构建的大规模真实世界事件数据集上取得了优异的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机在低光环境和严重运动模糊条件下表现不佳，事件相机虽具有高动态范围和高时间分辨率，但用于自我中心手部重建时，相机佩戴者运动产生的密集背景事件会掩盖手部信号，且此前方法无法区分左右手，导致预测错误和重建精度下降。

Method: 提出EventEgoHands++框架，包含两部分：1) Hand Detector（手部检测器）：估计左右手的实例级边界框和掩码；2) Adaptive Attention（自适应注意力）：根据检测结果动态门控注意力，准确学习双手间的空间关系和交互。训练数据方面扩展了合成N-HOT3D数据集并构建了EEH-R真实数据集。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法始终优于基线方法。

Conclusion: EventEgoHands++通过实例级手部检测和自适应注意力机制有效解决了事件相机自我中心手部重建中左右手区分和交互建模的难题，为低光等挑战环境下的3D手部重建提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: 3D hand mesh reconstruction is a challenging yet essential task for downstream applications, including human-robot interaction and AR/VR. Although conventional cameras have been widely adopted for this task, methods that rely on them struggle in low-light environments and under severe motion blur. To address these limitations, event-based cameras have recently attracted attention for their high dynamic range and high temporal resolution. However, applying event cameras to egocentric hand reconstruction remains challenging because camera wearer's motion produces dense background events that obscure hand-specific signals. Although the first egocentric event-based approach mitigates this issue using hand segmentation, its binary hand mask does not distinguish between left and right hands. As a result, the model lacks instance-level hand information and predicts both hands even when only one or neither hand is present. This limitation leads to incorrect inter-hand relationships and degraded reconstruction accuracy. In this paper, we propose EventEgoHands++, a framework for event-based 3D hand mesh reconstruction from an egocentric viewpoint. The proposed method incorporates a Hand Detector that estimates instance-level bounding boxes and masks for both the left and right hands. Moreover, we introduce Adaptive Attention, which dynamically gates the attention based on these detection results to accurately learn the spatial relationship and mutual interactions between the hands. To train and evaluate our framework, we extend the synthetic N-HOT3D dataset and newly construct EEH-R, the largest real-world event-based egocentric hand dataset to date, comprising approximately 1M annotated frames captured in environments including low-light conditions. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms the baselines.

</details>


### [78] [Probe-VAD: Ordinal Likelihood Probing for Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17211)
*Jiawei Gu,Qilin Zhao,Tengkuo Guo,Zhiming Zhong,Shuangqing Zhang,Fan Lyu,Fang Zhao,Guo-Sen Xie,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Probe-VAD是一种无需训练的序数二值探测框架，通过从冻结的视觉-语言模型中直接探测严重程度偏好来实现视频异常检测。该方法查询10个有序严重程度阈值，提取受约束的YES/NO延续概率，形成累积严重程度分布，聚合尾部证据生成连续异常分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM驱动的视频异常检测方法存在接口限制：基于描述的流程压缩视觉证据为文本可能丢失细微线索，而直接数值生成迫使模型通过预定义分数表达判断，掩盖异常严重程度的细微差异，导致视觉上不同的片段获得相似表示或分数，从而限制异常排序的分辨率。

Method: Probe-VAD提出序数二值探测框架，直接从冻结的VLM探测严重程度偏好。给定原始视频片段，查询10个有序严重程度阈值，提取受约束的YES/NO延续概率。归一化偏好形成累积严重程度分布，尾部证据聚合为连续异常分数，同构投影确保序数一致性。

Result: 在公共VAD基准测试上的实验表明，Probe-VAD以较低计算成本实现了优越性能。

Conclusion: Probe-VAD提供了一个简单接口，将冻结VLM的视觉理解转换为连续、对异常排序敏感的分数，无需任务特定训练或基于描述的压缩。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) aims to localize anomalous events in untrimmed videos. Vision-language models (VLMs) provide rich visual understanding for training-free VAD, but existing approaches impose restrictive interfaces between visual understanding and anomaly scoring. Caption-based pipelines compress visual evidence into text, potentially discarding subtle cues, while direct numerical generation forces the model to express its judgment through a small set of predefined scores. Such interfaces can obscure subtle differences in anomaly severity, causing visually distinct clips to receive similar representations or scores and thereby limiting the resolution of anomaly ranking. We propose \textbf{{Probe-VAD}}, an ordinal binary-probing framework that directly probes severity preferences from a frozen VLM. Given raw video clips, Probe-VAD queries ten ordered severity thresholds and extracts constrained \textit{{YES}}/\textit{{NO}} continuation likelihoods. Their normalized preferences form a cumulative severity profile, from which tail evidence is aggregated into a continuous anomaly score, with isotonic projection enforcing ordinal consistency. Experiments on public VAD benchmarks demonstrate superior performance with low computational cost. Probe-VAD provides a simple interface for translating frozen VLM visual understanding into continuous, rank-sensitive anomaly scores without task-specific training or caption-based compression. Code is available at: https://github.com/yvestine/COVAS-VAD.

</details>


### [79] [InfoTaxa: Information-Calibrated Label-Free Clustering for Fine-Grained Visual Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17218)
*David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Mohammad Ali Armin,Lars Petersson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究预训练视觉嵌入的无标签聚类在生物多样性监测中的应用，发现图像-only的细粒度分类存在"粗到细"失败模式：能恢复粗粒度分类结构但在物种级别停滞。通过InfoTaxa方法结合DNA作为审计信号分析，表明物种级无标签聚类同时受聚类算法限制和表示限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生物多样性监测的可扩展性问题，利用无标签聚类预训练视觉嵌入。但发现图像-only的细粒度分类在物种级别存在瓶颈，需要诊断这是方法限制还是信息限制。

Method: 使用BioCLIP特征结合UMAP和HDBSCAN进行聚类；引入InfoTaxa方法，结合聚类效率（无监督划分恢复的探针估计图像信息比例）和成对DNA作为审计信号（非推理输入）。进行晚融合探针测试等多角度鲁棒性分析。

Result: BioCLIP+UMAP+HDBSCAN在family级别达到0.79 AMI，genus级别0.67。密度管道在order和family级别分别恢复约0.90和0.81的图像可用信息。添加DNA将物种级预测误差降低约2 bits。

Conclusion: 在测试环境中，物种级无标签聚类同时受聚类限制和表示限制：改进聚类可恢复额外图像暴露结构，但无法仅凭此消除DNA审计的信息差距。

Abstract: Label-free clustering of frozen pretrained visual embeddings offers a scalable route to biodiversity monitoring, but image-only fine-grained taxonomy exhibits a consistent coarse-to-fine failure mode: clusters recover broad taxonomic structure yet plateau at species level. We study this behaviour on BIOSCAN-5M through an information-calibrated clustering analysis. BioCLIP~2 features with UMAP and HDBSCAN reach $0.79$ AMI at family and $0.67$ at genus, substantially improving over the prior image baseline and remaining competitive with oracle-$K$, graph-based, and learned clustering heads on the same frozen features. To diagnose whether the remaining plateau is method-limited or information-limited, we introduce InfoTaxa, which combines clustering efficiency---the fraction of probe-estimated image information recovered by an unsupervised partition---with paired DNA as an audit signal only, not an inference input. The density pipeline recovers approximately $0.90$ and $0.81$ of the image-available information at order and family, respectively. Held-out late-fusion probes show that adding DNA to the image embedding reduces species-level prediction error by approximately two bits. Robustness analyses cover multiple image encoders, described-species and rare-class subsets, probe diagnostics, and held-out-species coarse-rank generalisation and same-species retrieval. Thus, in the tested setting, species-level label-free clustering is both clustering-limited and representation-limited: improved clustering may recover additional image-exposed structure, but cannot close the DNA-audited information gap alone.

</details>


### [80] [FROD: Feature Matching Residual Denoising Oracle Bone Decipher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17227)
*Yanbin Hou,Biao Xiong,Guojun Xu,Jianwen Xiang,Cheng Tan,Yanchao Yang,Junwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FROD方法，将甲骨文识别任务建模为跨时代图像翻译问题，通过特征匹配提供门控分割监督，结合残差去噪扩散模型(RDDM)减少位置漂移和笔画紊乱，并使用多阶段字体风格化细化网络提升生成质量，在字符不重叠数据集上比OBSD提升3.8%的Top-1识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统甲骨文解读依赖领域专家通过语义上下文和结构演变进行分析，劳动密集且效率低下，需要自动化辅助工具来加速这一过程。

Method: 1) 快速特征匹配提供门控分割监督：高相似度样本采用逐patch对齐细粒度部首，低相似度样本采用整体训练避免错配；2) 残差去噪扩散模型(RDDM)联合估计噪声和残差信号；3) 多阶段字体风格化细化网络消除边缘噪声并稳定笔画结构。

Result: 在增强的字符不重叠数据集上，FROD获得更高的Top-1识别准确率，比OBSD提升3.8%的绝对增益。

Conclusion: 通过利用甲骨文字符在部首级别的局部拓扑不变量特性，结合特征匹配监督和残差去噪扩散模型，可以有效提升甲骨文识别的准确率，为古籍文献的自动解读提供了新思路。

Abstract: Oracle bone script (OBS), one of the earliest Chinese writing systems, plays an important role in the study of Chinese etymology. Traditional decipherment relies heavily on domain experts who analyze characters through semantic context and structural evolution. To assist this labor-intensive process, we formulate OBS decipherment assistance as a cross-era image translation task and propose FROD (Feature Matching Residual Denoising Oracle Bone Decipher). Although many OBS characters differ substantially from their modern counterparts, they often preserve local topological invariants at the radical level. During training, FROD leverages fast feature matching to provide gated segmentation supervision: paired samples with sufficient matches are processed patch-wise to align fine-grained radicals, whereas low-similarity pairs are trained holistically to avoid mismatched artifacts. In addition, a Residual Denoising Diffusion Model (RDDM) jointly estimates noise and residual signals, thereby reducing the positional drift and stroke disorder commonly observed in standard diffusion models. Finally, a multi-stage font stylization refinement network refines the generated images by eliminating edge noise and stabilizing stroke structures. On our augmented character-disjoint dataset, FROD achieves higher Top-1 recognition accuracy than the evaluated baselines, with a 3.8% absolute gain over OBSD.

</details>


### [81] [DecoGS: Adaptive Static-Dynamic Decoupling of 3D Gaussians for Free-Viewpoint Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17230)
*Idil Sulo,Alexey Supikov,Ilke Demir,Sainan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DecoGS是一种高效的流式视频3D高斯重建方法，通过自适应机制选择性地更新运动或光度变化区域，避免对静态区域的冗余更新，从而在保持时间一致性的同时实现快速、高保真的场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式3D重建方法在每一帧都更新所有高斯，即使在静态区域也是如此，导致效率低下且在静态区域产生闪烁和漂移问题。

Method: 1. 自适应机制：选择性聚焦于显示运动或光度变化的时空区域进行优化
2. 区域感知高斯管理：通过梯度门控实现
3. 高效可见性滤波：保持时间一致性和紧凑内存占用

Result: 在N3DV数据集上达到34.55 dB PSNR，在MeetRoom上达到31.60 dB PSNR；渲染速度261 FPS；时间闪烁比最佳先前方法低70倍；无需大规模预训练。

Conclusion: DecoGS在流式和离线基线方法中均取得最优性能，同时保持高渲染速度和时间一致性，为流式3D重建提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction demands both speed and temporal fidelity, goals that existing methods undermine by updating every Gaussian every frame, even in static regions. We present DecoGS, a method for efficient online training of 3D Gaussians from streaming videos. Unlike prior methods that update the entire scene indiscriminately, DecoGS introduces an adaptive mechanism that selectively focuses optimization on spatiotemporal regions exhibiting motion or photometric changes. This targeted training strategy eliminates redundant updates that cause flickering and drift in nominally static regions, while enabling fast, high-fidelity scene updates. The pipeline further integrates region-aware Gaussian management through gradient gating and efficient visibility filtering to maintain temporal coherence and a compact memory footprint. On N3DV and MeetRoom, DecoGS achieves 34.55 and 31.60 dB PSNR respectively, outperforming all streaming and offline baselines, while rendering at 261 FPS with $70\times$ lower temporal flicker than the best prior method, requiring no large-scale pretraining.

</details>


### [82] [Video-HolmesV2: Can MLLMs Reason with Spatio-Temporal Audio-Visual Evidence in Long Videos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17248)
*Zhaoyang Wei,Zipeng Wang,Yushe Cao,Chenhui Qiang,Shuaibing Cheng,Xuesong Yang,Sen Nie,Bowen Jiang,Wenchao Ding,Yanchao Hao,Zheng Wei,Xuehui Yu,Zhenjun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Video-HolmesV2是一个用于深度音频-视觉耦合的新型基准测试，旨在评估多模态大语言模型在长篇叙事视频中的推理能力。该基准通过证据驱动的评估方式，要求模型使用精确的时空音频-视觉证据来支持答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试过度依赖视觉线索，忽视听觉信息，将模型简化为"沉默观察者"；标准密集采样导致证据-上下文权衡问题，增加帧数会同时带来注意力分散和token爆炸；现有评估缺乏对跨模态推理的真实考察。

Method: 提出三方面创新：(1) Video-HolmesV2基准测试，支持深度音频-视觉耦合评估；(2) 多模型交叉验证管道确保任务严格性；(3) 时空证据感知指标实现细粒度校准。同时提出音频-文本引导的token压缩框架，融合任务意图与听觉锚点来提取高价值推理线索。

Result: 即使是最强的专有模型准确率也低于60%，而该方法优于可比的开源多模态模型。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了当前多模态模型在音频-视觉联合推理方面的不足，通过证据驱动的评估框架和token压缩技术，能够更有效地评估和提升模型在长视频理解任务中的表现。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have demonstrated impressive video understanding, yet their ability to reason over long-form narratives is often masked by visual-centric evaluations and inefficient context processing. Existing benchmarks over-rely on visual heuristics while marginalizing auditory cues, effectively reducing models to "silent observers" that bypass genuine cross-modal reasoning. Moreover, standard dense sampling creates an evidence-context trade-off: increasing frames to capture evidence inevitably leads to attention distraction and token explosion. To bridge these gaps, we present Video-HolmesV2, a novel benchmark designed for Deep Audio-Visual Coupling. Unlike previous works, it enforces an Evidence-Based Evaluation, requiring models to justify answers with precise spatio-temporal audio-visual evidence, thereby reducing confounding effects of guessing and hallucinated evidence. To support this, we introduce: (1) a Multi-Model Cross-Verification pipeline to ensure task rigor; (2) a Spatio-temporal Evidence-Aware Metric for fine-grained calibration. Furthermore, we propose an Audio-Text Guided Token Compression framework. By fusing task intent with auditory anchors, our method distills high-value reasoning cues to mitigate long-context noise. In our evaluation, even strong proprietary models achieve below 60% accuracy, while our approach outperforms comparable open-source omni-models.

</details>


### [83] [Exploring 2D backbone effects for indoor semantic occupancy prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17257)
*Shizhang Fanga,Wanling Yea,Qi Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究2D图像编码器对3D语义占用预测的影响，发现更换更强的编码器比改进3D架构更有效，DINOv2表现最佳（30.55% mIoU），远超原始ResNet-50基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决什么问题：RGB-D管道中图像编码器常被忽视，研究者想验证编码器选择对3D语义占用预测的影响

Method: 控制变量实验：固定RGB-D投影、深度分支和占用头，仅替换2D图像骨干网络，对比CLIP-ResNet、CLIP-ViT、BLIP2和DINOv2

Result: DINOv2达30.55% mIoU，BLIP2为29.49%，CLIP-ViT为24.33%，CLIP-ResNet为17.41%。强编码器无需修改下游3D融合即可超越原EmbodiedScan ResNet-50基线

Conclusion: 图像编码器是语义占用预测的关键影响因素，而非次要工程细节，应作为核心设计选择

Abstract: Semantic occupancy prediction gives an embodied agent a voxel-level account of where space is free, occupied, and semantically meaningful. In RGB-D pipelines such as EmbodiedScan, the image encoder is often left as a default module, even though its features are the visual evidence later sampled into the 3D grid. We study this design choice directly. A central finding is that changing the 2D backbone improves occupancy accuracy more than several carefully designed occupancy architectures or modules. We keep the main RGB-D projection, depth branch, and occupancy head fixed, and replace only the image backbone. The compared encoders are CLIP-ResNet, CLIP-ViT, BLIP2, and DINOv2. Under the controlled setting, the measured mIoU changes substantially: DINOv2 obtains 30.55\%, BLIP2 obtains 29.49\%, CLIP-ViT obtains 24.33\%, and CLIP-ResNet obtains 17.41\%. The stronger encoders also exceed the original EmbodiedScan ResNet-50 baseline without modifying the downstream 3D fusion pipeline. Class-level results give a more detailed picture: DINOv2 is stronger on many layout and structural categories, whereas BLIP2 remains close on several object-centered classes. CLIP-ViT improves clearly over CLIP-ResNet, showing that the way CLIP features are exposed as dense tokens matters for voxel lifting. These results indicate that the image backbone is not a secondary engineering detail in embodied semantic occupancy, but a major source of variation in the final 3D prediction.

</details>


### [84] [Semantic-Spatial Agreement Verification for Mitigating Object Hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17269)
*Ziheng Ren,Qian Gao,Jun Fan,Guohui Ding,Zhenyu Yang,Yuteng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SSAV（语义-空间一致性验证）方法，无需训练即可验证多模态大语言模型对图像中物体的声称，通过聚合多次提示估计语义支持并结合区域验证识别幻觉物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型可能产生物体幻觉（提及图像中不存在的物体），在药物辅助、无障碍感知和环境决策等场景中会造成真实安全风险，需要一种方法来验证模型声称的物体是否真实存在于图像中。

Method: SSAV包含两个分支：1）语义支持估计：聚合多种等价查询的响应来估计语义支持，降低对措辞的敏感性；2）QIRV（查询诱导区域验证）：结合跨查询区域持久性、空间重叠和相对候选优势识别孤立高响应和分散定位。使用几何平均融合语义和空间证据。 

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5-7B上，COCO、A-OKVQA和GQA的平均准确率在POPE Popular和Adversarial设置下分别提升1.81和3.17个百分点，CHAIRs从49.40%降至32.80%。

Conclusion: 跨查询语义稳定性和区域一致性为物体声称提供了可解释的外部视觉证据，SSAV能有效缓解多模态大语言模型中的物体幻觉问题。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models generate natural-language responses from visual inputs, yet may mention objects absent from an image. In medication assistance, accessible perception, and environmental decision-making, such hallucinations can create real-world safety risks. We propose Semantic-Spatial Agreement Verification (SSAV), a training-free method for verifying object claims. A visually grounded claim should remain stable across semantically equivalent queries and repeatedly localize to the same image region. SSAV aggregates multiple prompts to estimate semantic support and reduce sensitivity to query wording. Query-Induced Regional Verification (QIRV) combines cross-query region persistence, spatial overlap, and relative candidate dominance to identify isolated high responses and dispersed localizations. A geometric mean fuses semantic and spatial evidence, lowering the verification score when either branch lacks support. Experiments on three base models and multiple evaluation protocols show that SSAV effectively mitigates object hallucination. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, accuracy averaged across COCO, A-OKVQA, and GQA improves by 1.81 and 3.17 percentage points under POPE Popular and Adversarial, respectively, while CHAIRs decreases from 49.40% to 32.80%. These results show that cross-query semantic stability and regional consistency provide interpretable external visual evidence for object claims.

</details>


### [85] [Optical-Flow Wingbeat Counting in MuJoCo: A Comparison of Convolutional, Spiking, and Attention-Based Temporal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17308)
*Zhang Nengbo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在MuJoCo仿真环境中评估了基于光学流观测的扑翼飞行器翼拍计数方法，通过虚拟相机获取Crazyflie车辆的光学流数据，在1.5m和3.0m两种距离下测试了四种时序模型（因果卷积网络、循环LIF脉冲网络、自注意力机制），结合相位和活动度预测及事件计数器实现精确计数，各模型在两种距离下的准确率分别为96.67%/95.00%/96.67%和94.44%/92.22%/95.00%，各组间无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼飞行器的视觉监测需要从运动强度和平均频率中区分出单个翼拍周期，现有方法难以实现高精度计数，本研究旨在评估基于光学流的翼拍计数可行性并比较不同神经网络架构的性能。

Method: 采用MuJoCo仿真平台构建三种扑翼模型，在1.5m和3.0m光学距离下通过虚拟相机获取签名光学流，生成了240个配对场景配置共1440个视频片段，采用3:1训练测试分割。将空间卷积编码器与因果时序卷积网络、循环漏积分-发射脉冲神经网络或因果自注意力三种时序模型分别结合，各模型输出相位和活动度预测，再通过有向穿越事件计数器生成最终计数结果。

Result: 1.5m距离时三种模型精确计数准确率分别为96.67%、95.00%和96.67%；3.0m距离时分别为94.44%、92.22%和95.00%。所有配对场景的自助置信区间均包含零，表明无显著差异。七个远距离脉冲模型视频片段虽存在事件时间错配但总数正确，说明需要同时报告总数和事件级测量结果。

Conclusion: 研究验证了在测试环境中基于因果光学流计数的可行性并识别出边界敏感误差，但未能在重复训练、真实飞行鲁棒性或硬件效率方面建立架构排名，需进一步研究以确定最佳方案。

Abstract: Visual monitoring of flapping-wing vehicles requires distinguishing individual wingbeats from motion strength and average frequency. This paper presents a controlled MuJoCo evaluation of wingbeat counting from signed optical flow observed by virtual cameras mounted on Crazyflie vehicles. Three flapping-wing models were recorded at optical distances of 1.5 and 3.0 m, producing 1,440 clips from 240 paired scene configurations with a scene-level 3:1 training-test split. A common spatial convolutional encoder was combined with a causal temporal convolutional network, a recurrent leaky integrate-and-fire spiking network, or causal self-attention. Each model predicted phase and activity, followed by the same directed-crossing event counter. The six existing convolutional models were retained, and all twelve new models were frozen before their test predictions were generated. Exact-count accuracies at 1.5 m were 96.67%, 95.00%, and 96.67%, respectively; at 3.0 m they were 94.44%, 92.22%, and 95.00%. All paired scene-bootstrap intervals for differences in exact-count accuracy included zero. Seven far-distance spiking-model clips had correct totals despite event-timing mismatches, demonstrating why total-count and event-level measurements must be reported together. The results support the feasibility of causal optical-flow counting in the tested setting and identify boundary-sensitive errors. They do not establish an architecture ranking across repeated training, real-flight robustness, or hardware efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [PanoGS-SLAM: Panoramic 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17387)
*Yongqi Mao,Hao Shi,Yufan Zhang,Zhonghua Yi,Xiangfei Guo,Kaiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoGS-SLAM是首个基于3D高斯溅射的全景密集SLAM系统，通过在球域中进行可微渲染和姿态优化，实现更稳定的跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-SLAM方法专为窄视角针孔相机设计，有限的角覆盖率削弱了姿态可观测性，在快速运动和大视角变化下往往导致光度优化不稳定。

Method: 1. 在球域中进行可微渲染和姿态优化，实现全方位光度约束；2. 提出球面一致性光度损失，补偿等距柱状投影的面积畸变；3. 引入深度引导的高斯初始化策略，稳定新观测区域的增量建图。

Result: 在PALVIO和SynPano全景基准数据集上，追踪精度和渲染质量均优于几何和GS基线方法，实现快速前端收敛和实时性能。控制实验表明角覆盖率增加时优化条件单调改善。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了传感几何在塑造可微高斯SLAM优化景观中的基础作用，证明了全景视角对系统稳定性的关键贡献。

Abstract: Real-time dense SLAM is a core capability for robotics applications that require robust localization and high- quality mapping in dynamic or fast-changing environments. Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-based SLAM methods have shown promising performance, but most are designed for narrow-FoV pinhole cameras, where limited angular coverage weakens pose observability and often leads to unstable photo- metric optimization under rapid motion and large viewpoint changes. We present PanoGS-SLAM, the first panoramic dense SLAM system built on 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our method per- forms differentiable rendering and pose optimization directly in the spherical domain, enabling omnidirectional photometric constraints for more stable tracking. To improve geometric consistency and robustness, we introduce (1) a sphere-consistent photometric loss that compensates for the area distortion of equirectangular projection, and (2) a depth-guided Gaussian initialization strategy that stabilizes incremental mapping in newly observed regions. Extensive experiments on both real and synthetic panoramic benchmarks (PALVIO and SynPano) show that PanoGS-SLAM consistently outperforms geometric and GS-based baselines in tracking accuracy and rendering quality, while achieving fast front-end convergence and real-time perfor- mance. In addition, controlled field-of-view experiments reveal a clear monotonic improvement in optimization conditioning and convergence stability as angular coverage increases, high- lighting the fundamental role of sensing geometry in shaping the optimization landscape of differentiable Gaussian-based SLAM. The source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [87] [SSC-Priors: Exploring Semantic and Visibility Priors to Boost Lidar Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17413)
*Tetiana Martyniuk,Jonathan Seele,Alexandre Boulch,Gilles Puy,Renaud Marlet,Raoul de Charette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索了两种简单策略（语义伪标签和传感器可见性信息）来提升现有激光雷达语义场景补全网络的性能，无需复杂架构重新设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，语义场景补全（SSC）方法大多追求架构创新，导致模型日益复杂笨重。本文旨在探索简单的先验信息作为额外输入来改进现有方法。

Method: 1）将语义伪标签（来自现成分割器）直接作为输入添加到点云中；2）添加可见性信息以区分空旷空间（激光雷达与扫描点之间）和未知空间（视线外）。仅需对原架构进行最小化修改。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI上，这些改进使四种架构的老模型达到与最先进系统相当的水平，其中一种甚至超越了最先进系统。在SSCBench-nuScenes（32-beam稀疏传感器）上也能有效迁移。

Conclusion: 高质量语义先验是语义增益（mIoU）的主要驱动因素；可见性信息提供了额外的性能提升。这两种先验可显著提升现有SSC模型的竞争力，且无需大规模重新训练。

Abstract: This paper investigates easy strategies to boost the performance of existing networks for lidar semantic scene completion (SSC) without requiring complex architectural redesigns. The fact is that, over the last years, SSC methods have mostly pursued architectural innovations, making the models heavier and more complex, e.g., by jointly training a point cloud semantic segmentation branch. In this work, we take a step back and explore two priors used as simple ingredients (possibly noisy) to improve existing approaches: semantic pseudo-labels and sensor visibility information. Concretely, we provide both kinds of information directly as additional inputs to a given SSC network, requiring only a minimal adaptation of the original architecture. We first demonstrate that endowing input point clouds with semantic pseudo-labels from off-the-shelf segmenters significantly improves the performance of existing SSC models. In fact, by evaluating these models against an oracle, we establish that high-quality semantic priors are a primary driver of semantic gains (mIoU), and that the SSC model can be trained just once with ground-truth semantics and then exploited without retraining using any segmenter. Furthermore, we equip the input lidar point cloud with visibility information that distinguishes between empty spaces (between the lidar and a scanned point) and unknown spaces (outside of lines of sight), providing a secondary performance boost across the tested architectures. We study the design space of data for representing visibility information and bound the remaining headroom with a ground-truth oracle on the free-space labels. On SemanticKITTI, these enhancements make older models competitive with state-of-the-art systems across four architectures, in one case even outperforming them. On the SSCBench-nuScenes benchmark, both priors also transfer with the sparser 32-beam sensor.

</details>


### [88] [SlotDiT: Object-Centric Representations for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17414)
*Gjergj Plepi,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SlotDiT，一个基于槽（slot）的文本引导扩散Transformer（DiT），用于机器人场景的视频生成和动态预测。首次将基于槽的目标中心表示应用于扩散生成模型，并在统一框架下与VAE和语义对齐表示进行系统比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件潜在扩散模型在视频生成和机器人应用中表现良好，但依赖于缺乏明确语义结构的像素级或VAE潜在表示。槽式目标中心表示提供了一种结构化替代方案，可将场景分解为对象级潜在，但尚未在扩散生成建模中探索。

Method: SlotDiT给定参考图像和语言指令，将场景分解为表示单个实体的对象中心槽。模型在指令和观察到的场景上下文条件下，自回归地去噪未来槽轨迹以预测场景动态。为系统研究扩散变换器的潜在空间设计，在统一DiT框架下比较槽基表示与VAE基和语义对齐表示。

Result: 使用槽作为DiT潜在在视频生成质量上具有竞争力，同时在四个机器人数据集上持续提高任务完成率。其紧凑表示比VAE基和语义对齐潜在空间更具计算效率。

Conclusion: 对象中心结构是机器人环境中扩散生成建模的强大归纳偏置。槽基表示在保持竞争力的生成质量同时，能显著提升下游机器人任务的完成率。

Abstract: Text-conditioned latent diffusion models perform strongly in video generation and are promising backbones for robotic applications. However, existing approaches rely on pixel-level or VAE-based latent representations that lack explicit semantic structure, leaving the impact of the representation space largely unexplored. Slot-based object-centric representations offer a structured alternative by decomposing scenes into object-level latents, or slots. While they have shown success in dynamics modeling and planning, they have not yet been explored for diffusion-based generative modeling. We introduce SlotDiT, a text-guided Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates in a slot-based latent space. Given a reference image and a language instruction, SlotDiT decomposes the scene into object-centric slots representing individual entities. Conditioned on the instruction and observed scene context, the model autoregressively denoises future slot trajectories to predict scene dynamics. To systematically investigate latent-space design for diffusion transformers, we compare slot-based representations against VAE-based and semantics-aligned alternatives within a unified DiT framework. Our experiments show that using slots as DiT latents yields competitive video generation quality while consistently improving task-completion rates across four robotic datasets. Furthermore, their compact representation provides a computationally efficient alternative to VAE-based and semantics-aligned latent spaces. Overall, our results demonstrate that object-centric structure is a powerful inductive bias for diffusion-based generative modeling in robotic environments. The project page is available at https://slot-dit.github.io/.

</details>


### [89] [Tracking the Unseen: An Occlusion-Robust Framework for Target Tracking Under Full and Long-Term Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17427)
*Mais Mohammed,Sharifa Mohammed,Hanan Awadh,Haneen Bamaas,Raghad Bawazeer,Elham Alghamdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种遮挡鲁棒的多目标跟踪框架，通过集成YOLOv11n检测器、卡尔曼滤波和遮挡感知重识别(OAMN)，在长期遮挡场景下维持目标身份和轨迹连续性，在OVIS和军事数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时多目标跟踪系统容易受到完全和长期遮挡的影响，目标暂时或完全离开相机视野，导致轨迹过早终止、身份丢失和态势感知下降， defense 和 surveillance 应用受影响。

Method: 提出了一种遮挡鲁棒目标跟踪框架，集成YOLOv11n目标检测、卡尔曼滤波运动预测和遮挡感知外观重识别技术。框架包含三个阶段：目标检测、遮挡期间位置估计和目标重现后身份恢复。在相同条件下评估了六种重识别架构，选择表现最佳的遮挡感知掩码网络(OAMN)。

Result: 在OVIS数据集上，相比OccluTrack，MOTA相对提升18.1%，IDF1相对提升25.1%，身份切换减少12.8%。在自定义军事数据集上，MOTA达0.734，IDF1达0.729，分别相对提升14.2%和5.8%。

Conclusion: 该框架在遮挡条件下展现出强大的跟踪连续性、身份保留和轨迹估计能力，有效支持国防相关 surveillance 应用的长期遮挡目标跟踪需求。

Abstract: Real-time multi-object tracking systems remain highly vulnerable to full and long-term occlusion, where targets temporarily or completely disappear from the camera's field of view. Conventional trackers may terminate trajectories prematurely, resulting in identity loss and reduced situational awareness in applications such as defense and surveillance. This work proposes an occlusion-robust target tracking framework that maintains target identity and trajectory continuity through the integration of YOLOv11n object detection, Kalman Filter motion prediction, and occlusion-aware appearance-based re-identification. The framework consists of three stages: object detection, position estimation during occlusion, and identity recovery after target reappearance. Six Re-Identification (Re-ID) architectures were evaluated within the same tracking framework under identical conditions, with the Occlusion-Aware Mask Network (OAMN) achieving the best overall performance and therefore selected for the final pipeline. The framework was benchmarked against OccluTrack on the public OVIS dataset, achieving relative improvements of 18.1 percent in Multiple Object Tracking Accuracy (MOTA) and 25.1 percent in Identity F1 Score (IDF1), while reducing identity switches by 12.8 percent. On a custom military dataset simulating surveillance and battlefield-like environments with long-term occlusion, the framework achieved a MOTA of 0.734 and an IDF1 of 0.729, corresponding to relative improvements of 14.2 percent and 5.8 percent over OccluTrack. The system demonstrated strong tracking continuity, robust identity preservation, and reliable trajectory estimation under challenging occlusion conditions, highlighting its effectiveness for defense-related surveillance applications requiring continuous target tracking during visibility loss.

</details>


### [90] [BrainFocus: EEG-Guided ROI Selection for Efficient Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17443)
*Yihui Peng,Guorui Lu,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainFocus是一个利用脑电图(EEG)信号引导的高效视觉问答(VQA)框架,可在EEG语义解码不完美的情况下，通过EEG预测目标类别并结合YOLO检测器定位感兴趣区域，从而减少VLM的输入token和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉问答模型处理大型杂乱图像计算成本高，而EEG信号可提供人类对感兴趣区域的语义线索，但EEG引导的视觉类别解码仍不完善，使得直接ROI路由不可靠。

Method: EEG分类器预测目标类别，YOLO检测器定位匹配ROI。当两者预测都通过置信度阈值时，VLM仅接收裁剪的ROI；否则处理完整图像。在EEG-ImageNet上构建40类基准测试，包含生成的杂乱图像和真实目标图像，附带ROI标注和600对英文问答。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-VL 2B、4B和9B模型上，BrainFocus在杂乱场景下将VQA准确率提升4.14-9.87个百分点，输入token和总token减少23.2%-39.4%和23.2%-39.3%，端到端FLOPs减少23.2%-39.5%。

Conclusion: 即使EEG语义解码不完美，EEG仍可有效引导VLM高效推理，证明了人机协作在降低计算成本方面的潜力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong visual question answering (VQA) performance, but processing large cluttered images is computationally expensive when only a small region is relevant. Electroencephalography (EEG) signals, which capture human neural responses to visual stimuli, can provide a human-derived semantic cue about the region of interest (ROI). However, EEG-guided visual category decoding remains imperfect, making direct ROI routing unreliable. In this work, we propose BrainFocus, a reliable EEG-guided efficient VLM framework for VQA. An EEG classifier predicts a target category, and a YOLO detector localizes the matching ROI. The VLM receives the cropped ROI only when both predictions pass confidence thresholds; otherwise, it processes the full image. For evaluation, we build on EEG-ImageNet to construct a 40-class benchmark comprising generated cluttered images and real object-centric images, with target-ROI annotations and 600 English visual question-answer pairs. Across Qwen3.5-VL 2B, 4B, and 9B models, BrainFocus improves VQA accuracy by 4.14-9.87 percentage points (pp) on cluttered scenes while reducing input tokens and total tokens by 23.2%-39.4% and 23.2%-39.3%, and end-to-end floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 23.2%-39.5%. These results demonstrate that EEG can guide efficient VLM inference even when its semantic decoding is imperfect.

</details>


### [91] [ORCA: Occlusion-Aware Refinement and Completion for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17450)
*Weronika Jakubowska,Maciej Zięba,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORCA是一种基于单张图像进行可探索3D场景重建和补全的方法，通过区分小规模遮挡区域（利用已有RGB-D信息修复）和大面积缺失区域（使用生成式修复），减少了对生成模型的过度依赖，从而更好地保留原始场景内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像新视角合成存在根本性歧义，当相机移动时先前被遮挡的区域变得可见，暴露缺失的几何结构。现有方法通常依赖生成模型来补全这些区域，但许多缺失是小范围的深度边界附近的孔洞，不需要生成新内容。

Method: ORCA首先使用单目深度将3D结构引入高斯锚点表示，同时保留原始相机射线对应关系。在场景探索阶段，根据缺失区域的大小和结构进行处理：小范围遮挡使用重建中已有的RGB-D信息修复，大范围区域使用生成式修复。新增的高斯锚点在局部进行优化，不修改现有表示。

Result: 在DIV2K数据集上，ORCA在所有指标上超越VistaDream：MUSIQ从61.60提升至68.71，CLIP-IQA从0.474提升至0.574。

Conclusion: 许多新视角伪影可以通过重用重建场景中已有的信息进行有效修复，减少对生成式修复的依赖可以限制生成引起的幻觉，更好地保留原始场景的内容和结构。

Abstract: Novel-view synthesis from a single image is a fundamentally ambiguous problem. As the camera moves away from the input viewpoint, previously hidden regions become visible, exposing missing geometry and holes in the reconstructed scene. Existing methods often rely on generative models to complete such regions. However, many of these artifacts are small gaps near depth boundaries and do not require generating new scene content.
  In order to eliminate expensive process of generating image we introduce ORCA, an occlusion-aware method for reconstructing and completing explorable 3D scenes from a single image. ORCA first introduces 3D structure into a Gaussian-anchor representation using monocular depth while preserving the original camera-ray correspondence. During scene exploration, missing regions are handled based on their size and structure. Small disocclusions are repaired using RGB-D information already available in the reconstruction, while generative inpainting is reserved for larger regions that cannot be reliably recovered from the scene. New Gaussian anchors are added and optimized locally without modifying the existing representation. By reducing unnecessary reliance on generative inpainting, ORCA limits generation-induced hallucinations and better preserves the content and structure of the original scene.
  On DIV2K, ORCA improves novel-view quality over VistaDream across all reported metrics, increasing MUSIQ from 61.60 to 68.71 and CLIP-IQA from 0.474 to 0.574. These results show that many novel-view artifacts can be repaired effectively by reusing information already present in the reconstructed scene.

</details>


### [92] [Det-LIME: Detector-Aware, Multi-Instance Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations for Automated Marine Mammal Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17479)
*Jiayi Zhou,David W. Johnston,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对海洋哺乳动物研究中使用黑盒目标检测器缺乏可解释性的问题，提出了Det-LIME方法，这是一种针对检测任务的多实例LIME适配器，能够生成实例特定、边界框对齐的解释。实验表明该方法在海豹检测和海鸟检测任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋哺乳动物研究和监测中快速采用了黑盒目标检测器，但可解释性技术很少被整合到保护工作流程中。现有的分类导向可解释性工具不适合处理社会性生物或群体生活史生物的图像检测任务，因为它们忽略了场景中的多个检测，且产生的单实例输出会在个体间模糊证据。此外，这些方法生成的图像分辨率低且往往与生物学无关，限制了其在调试、针对性数据增强和精细数据收集方面的效用。

Method: 提出了Det-LIME方法，这是Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations (LIME)的检测器感知多实例适配器。该方法通过以下方式生成实例特定、边界框对齐的解释：(1)每检测加权机制；(2)强调每个边界框附近区域的邻近核函数；(3)基于Intersection-over-Union (IoU)的匹配方法，以在扰动中跟踪同一实例。在无人机航拍的港口海豹检测图像上评估了Det-LIME，并辅以海鸟案例研究来评估其通用性。

Result: 使用Attribution Ratio和Max Saliency Hit Rate指标进行评估，结果表明Det-LIME始终改善了多实例归因。在实际应用中，这些高分辨率、实例感知的解释能够深入了解模型输出，支持后处理、调试，以及在建模和数据收集或增强方面的可操作改进。

Conclusion: Det-LIME成功解决了现有可解释性方法在目标检测任务中的局限性，特别是多实例场景下的解释问题。该方法生成的实例级、高分辨率解释具有实际应用价值，可支持海洋哺乳动物监测中的模型调试和改进，为保护工作流程中的可解释人工智能应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Despite the rapid uptake of black-box object detectors in marine mammal research and monitoring, explainability techniques are rarely integrated into conservation workflows. Furthermore, most classification-oriented explainability tools are ill-suited to detection tasks involving imagery of social organisms or those with colonial life histories, as they ignore multiple detections within a scene and produce single-instance outputs that blur evidence across individuals. These methods also generate low-resolution, often biologically irrelevant visuals, limiting their utility for debugging, targeted data augmentation, and refined data collection.
  We proposed Det-LIME, a detector-aware, multi-instance adaptation of Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations (LIME) that produced instance-specific, box-aligned explanations by combining per-detection weighting, a proximity kernel that emphasizes regions near each box, and Intersection-over-Union-based matching to track the same instance across perturbations. We evaluated Det-LIME on aerial drone imagery for harbor seal detection, with an additional seabird case study to assess generality, and compared it with vanilla LIME, Stabilized LIME, Deterministic LIME, and gradient-based attribution methods.
  Using the Attribution Ratio and Max Saliency Hit Rate metrics, we showed that Det-LIME consistently improved multi-instance attribution. In practice, these higher-resolution, instance-aware explanations provide insight into model outputs and support post-processing, debugging, and actionable improvements in modeling and data collection or augmentation.

</details>


### [93] [Tables Decoded: DELTA for Structure, TARQA for Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17458)
*Jahanvi Rajput,Dhruv Kudale,Saikiran Kasturi,Utkarsh Verma,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DELTA和TARQA两个模型，基于结构化文本表示（OTSL格式）实现表格重建和表格视觉问答任务，替代传统的视觉语言模型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格理解主要依赖视觉语言模型(VLM)，存在视觉编码器依赖语言、难以扩展到多语言文档的问题，作者希望提出更可扩展且有效的方法。

Method: 1) DELTA分离物理结构识别、逻辑结构识别和OCR，从表格图像中提取布局和内容；2) 输出为OTSL（优化表格结构语言）格式；3) TARQA是基于OTSL序列微调的大型语言模型。

Result: 1) TSR任务：在FinTabNet、PubTabNet、PubTables-1M上TEDS-Structure得分与SOTA方法相当；2) 非英文表格：在Hindi基准TORQUE上表现鲁棒；3) TabQA任务：WTQ提升9.3个百分点，FinTabNetQA提升9.2个百分点；4) TORQUE排名：位列所有VLM和DELTA+LLM变体第二。

Conclusion: 基于结构化文本表示的方法可有效替代纯视觉方法，更易处理、与LLM更自然对齐、适合多语言文档，且在表格重建和问答任务上取得了竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Table understanding is a core task in document intelligence, encompassing two key subtasks: table reconstruction and table visual question answering (TabVQA). While recent approaches predominantly rely on vision- language models (VLMs) operating on table images, we propose a more scalable and effective alternative based on structured textual representations. These representations are easier to process, align more naturally with LLMs, and eliminate the need for language-specific visual encoders, making them particularly suitable for multilingual documents. We present DELTA, which separates physical structure recognition, logical structure recognition, and OCR to extract both layout and content accurately. DELTA outputs tables in Optimised Table Structure Language (OTSL), a compact and unified format that encodes cell arrangements and textual content. On table structure recognition (TSR), DELTA achieves TEDS- Structure scores comparable with state-of-the-art methods across FinTabNet, PubTabNet, and PubTables-1M. We further establish its robustness on non-English tables through our curated Hindi benchmark, TORQUE. Building on this, we introduce TARQA, an LLM fine-tuned on OTSL sequences. Our approach yields gains of 9.3 p.p. on WTQ (TabQA) and 9.2 p.p. on FinTabNetQA (TabVQA), respectively. On TORQUE, our method ranks second among all VLMs and DELTA + LLM variants. We release our code, models, and benchmark at: https://github.com/Tihiitborg/Tables-Decoded

</details>


### [94] [PhysStream: Streaming Physics-Grounded Video Generation with Structured Scene Memory and Fine-Grained Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17521)
*Chuhao Chen,Peter Wonka,Chaoyang Wang,Chen Wang,Qiao Feng,Sergey Tulyakov,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysStream是一个用于物理接地图像到视频合成的自回归模型，通过结构化场景记忆（位置图和对象跟踪图）和稀疏速度增量信号实现细粒度运动控制，支持多对象刚体场景的交互式中间生成控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频生成方法存在局限性：要么需要在生成开始前提供完整控制计划，要么使用像素空间信号来指定对象位置而非物理动力学。为解决这些限制，提出PhysStream来支持细粒度、物理意义的动态场景操作。

Method: PhysStream采用两阶段训练：首先对双向模型进行运动控制条件微调，然后训练因果自回归模型并加入额外结构化场景记忆。模型使用在线从已生成帧中提取的位置图和对象跟踪图作为结构化场景记忆，通过稀疏速度增量信号编码物理量来实现运动控制。

Result: 在合成基准测试中，PhysStream将运动分布距离(FVMD)降低33%，轨迹误差降低12%，优于最强基线方法。在野外比较中，人类评估者85%以上的情况更偏好该方法。

Conclusion: PhysStream实现了多对象桌面刚体场景的交互式中间生成控制，这是先前方法不支持的能力，为视频生成的细粒度物理控制开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Interactive control for video generation is moving from coarse prompts toward fine-grained, physically meaningful manipulation of dynamic scenes. Yet existing controllable methods either require the full control schedule before generation starts, or use pixel-space signals that dictate object positions rather than physical dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose PhysStream, an autoregressive model for physics-grounded image-to-video synthesis that incorporates structured scene memory---positional maps and object tracking maps derived online from previously generated frames---and supports fine-grained motion control via sparse velocity-increment signals that encode physical quantities, letting the model learn the underlying dynamics. We train our model in two stages: a bidirectional model is first finetuned with motion-control conditioning, then a causal autoregressive model is trained with additional structured scene memory, further improving physical consistency. PhysStream enables interactive, mid-generation control over multi-object tabletop rigid-body scenes---a capability not supported by prior methods---reducing motion distribution distance (FVMD) by 33% and trajectory error by 12% over the strongest baselines on synthetic benchmarks, and is preferred by human evaluators in over 85% of in-the-wild comparisons. Please check our website for more details: https://czzzzh.github.io/PhysStream

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] ["Looking for Something Weird to Happen": How Humans Sustain AI Agent Novelty Amid Semantic Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16051)
*Shiyang Lai,Arna Woemmel,Hongkai Mao,Junsol Kim,Summer Eunhyung Ann,James Evans*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文研究AI社交网络MOLTBOOK中的语义坍塌现象，发现多数AI智能体输出多样性随时间下降，但少数智能体保持高新颖性，通过用户访谈和调查揭示了维持新颖性的三个关键用户行为特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前对语义坍塌的研究主要在封闭环境中进行，解决方案针对模型和数据。本文认为人类用户的交互方式也可能是导致或缓解语义坍塌的关键因素，需要在开放的社会网络环境中研究这一现象。

Method: 对30,076个活跃智能体进行大规模数据分析，追踪其输出多样性的时间变化；对高新颖性和典型新颖性智能体的用户进行深度访谈（N=11）；对独特智能体用户进行问卷调查（N=53）；分析社区层面新颖性对整体输出多样性的影响。

Result: 发现智能体内输出多样性随时间下降，智能体之间输出相似性增加，但约15%的智能体维持高新颖性。访谈和调查确认三个关键特征：用户重视新颖性本身、提供广泛独特材料并在输出变窄时主动修改、将智能体视为探索对象而非工具。有更多新颖智能体的社区其他智能体输出也更丰富样。

Conclusion: 语义坍塌不仅是技术问题，也与人类用户的交互方式密切相关。界面设计和政策干预应鼓励用户提供更丰富多样的输入、及时修正AI输出、并以探索性而非工具性心态使用AI系统。

Abstract: Semantic collapse, the progressive narrowing of what AI systems generate, has been studied mainly in closed settings, and remedies have targeted models and data. We study it in MOLTBOOK, a social network of interacting AI agents that human users configure and steer. Across 30,076 active agents, output grows less diverse within agents and more similar across them over weeks, yet a minority sustains high novelty. Interviews with users of high- and typical-novelty agents (N=11) associate sustained novelty with three features: users value novelty of itself, they supply broad and distinctive material and revise it when output narrows, and they approach MOLTBOOK as a new agentic world to explore, not a venue to instrumentally exploit. A survey of users of distinctive agents (N=53) confirms these patterns. Communities with more novel agents also show more diverse output from other agents. We discuss interface and policy interventions that could support improved human input.

</details>


### [96] [Cheap Talk Stabilizes Strategic Interaction in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16270)
*Nunzio Lorè,Hongan Zhu,Babak Heydari*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究大型语言模型作为交互智能体时，非约束性预发通信（"cheap talk"）是否能增强其在重复交互中的策略持久性。实验覆盖四款7-9B参数开源模型和四种重复两人博弈，结果显示cheap talk总体具有稳定化作用，但其效果因模型和情境而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地被部署为交互智能体，但其动作策略在重复交互中的持久性对多智能体系统的可靠性至关重要。之前研究缺乏对LLM智能体在长期交互中策略稳定性以及通信对稳定性影响的系统分析。

Method: 在四款开源LLM（7-9B参数）上测试四种重复两人博弈（囚徒困境、雪堆博弈、 stag hunt、 Harmony），每个游戏设置六种情境。通过控制当前消息干预、匹配历史-消息反事实分析以及神经网络层投影等方法，识别稳定化机制和因果敏感成分。

Result: 所有四种游戏中都观察到不稳定轨迹，但不稳定程度因模型和情境而异。总体上cheap talk主要起稳定化作用，五个纠正的逆转集中在社会或团队框架中。Qwen模型中识别出两个可分离的输出层通道：减少动作不确定性和减少轮次间动作概率漂移。反事实分析显示近期伙伴行为调节了互惠优先与自我优先语言对策略持久性的影响。在囚徒困境中，Qwen和Falcon的晚期transformer层存在历史平衡的策略内容方向，投影去除该方向会增加闭环执行中的策略切换。

Conclusion: Cheap talk能使个体轨迹在多样激励结构下更加持久，但稳定化的程度和机制依赖于模型和历史状态。研究揭示了LLM智能体策略稳定性的重要影响因素，为构建更可靠的多智能体系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as interacting agents, making the persistence of their action policies across repeated interaction critical for reliable multi-agent operation. We investigate whether and how agent-generated, non-binding pre-play communication ("cheap talk") increases such persistence in four open-weight 7-9B-parameter LLMs. Our experiments span four repeated two-player games -- Prisoner's Dilemma, Snowdrift, Stag Hunt, and Harmony -- with incentive structures ranging from strategic conflict to alignment, each presented in six contexts. We observe unstable trajectories in all four games, although their prevalence and magnitude depend strongly on model and context. Across models, games, and contexts, cheap talk is predominantly stabilizing, with five corrected reversals concentrated in social or team framings; effects vary substantially by model and context. Controlled current-message interventions identify two separable output-level channels in Qwen: reduced action uncertainty and less between-round drift in action probabilities. Matched history-by-message counterfactuals further show that recent partner behavior conditions how mutual-benefit versus self-prioritizing language affects policy persistence. Finally, in Prisoner's Dilemma, we identify in Qwen and Falcon a history-balanced policy-content direction in late transformer layers; projecting out this direction increases realized switching during closed-loop play, demonstrating that complete trajectories are causally sensitive to this component. Together, these findings show that cheap talk can make individual trajectories more persistent across diverse incentive structures, while revealing that the magnitude and mechanisms of stabilization are model- and history-dependent.

</details>


### [97] [Multi-Agent Learning with Cooperation-Driven Optimization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16917)
*Jarod Ketcha Kouakep,Sreyvi UANN,Timoteo Carletti*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出一种多智能体协作训练机制，通过多个小型神经网络协作来减少参数数量同时保持分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络虽然能以任意精度逼近任何函数，但需要大量参数导致计算资源消耗大，希望通过多智能体协作来降低模型复杂度。

Method: 让多个小型智能体在训练时共享预测信息，将协作信号融入损失函数影响权重更新，采用投票模型、多数模型和置信度加权平均模型三种策略。

Result: 多个小型智能体在标准基准数据集上可以优于单个大型模型，共享信号通过调制下降方向和步长使各智能体趋向全局共识，显著减少训练参数数量。

Conclusion: 提出的协作机制能在大幅减少参数的情况下保持与大型模型相当的性能，为降低计算资源消耗提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multilayer Artificial Neural Networks trained via backpropagation are the basic blocks of many, more complex, classification algorithms. Their strength lies in the possibility of realizing, with arbitrary precision, any function. This result comes at the cost of the large number of involved parameters to be optimized. In this work, we propose a mechanism for cooperation, i.e., information exchange among several artificial neural networks, with the goal of reducing model complexity while maintaining performance. More precisely, we consider several "small" agents, i.e., containing fewer parameters than a reference "large" one, that during training share their predictions by incorporating this information into the loss function and thus directly influence weight updates. We consider several strategies for implementing cooperation, e.g., the voter model, majority model, and weighted average model based on an agent's confidence in its prediction. We numerically compare the accuracy of those strategies on several standard benchmarks. Our results support the claim that several small agents can outperform a single large model on a given classification task; the shared signals affect each agent's optimization algorithm by modulating both the descent direction and the step size, converging toward a global consensus. The proposed proof-of-concept significantly reduces the number of parameters to be trained while preserving comparable performance, thereby limiting computational resource usage.

</details>


### [98] [ToMAS: A Pilot Failure-Grounded Theory-of-Mind Benchmark from Multi-Agent LLM Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16986)
*Muhammad Ashar Ishfaq,Glaucia Melo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出ToMAS框架，将多智能体系统中的协调失败（FC2）转换为可训练的对等方状态推理项目。通过对242条非AG2训练轨迹进行转换，产生39个CLEAN项目。在18条轨迹的可靠性试点中，两名标注者达到94.4%的一致率和Cohen's kappa=0.92。使用转换后的项目作为Qwen2.5-1.5B的二元奖励进行小规模GRPO可行性实验，但在Magentic GAIA诊断集上所有条件解码结果与未训练模型完全相同，未能显示训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统即使通信成功也可能失败，原因是智能体无法正确跟踪同伴的角色、知识或意图。这种智能体间错位情况（标记为FC2）需要被转化为可训练的对等方状态推理项目。

Method: ToMAS应用四个明确的可转换性标准来诊断执行轨迹。进行完整转换处理242条符合条件的非AG2训练轨迹，产生39个CLEAN项目。开展18条轨迹的可靠性试点研究。使用转换项目作为二元奖励进行Qwen2.5-1.5B的小规模GRPO可行性实验。

Result: 可靠性试点中两名标注者达到94.4%的原始一致率和Cohen's kappa=0.92。但GRPO实验显示所有条件在28项Magentic GAIA诊断集上与未训练模型解码结果完全相同，因为LoRA更新数值上可忽略（最大绝对Delta W约7e-6），未能建立训练效果。

Conclusion: ToMAS提供了将诊断的协调失败转换为可训练对等方状态推理项目的初步框架和流程，并确定了任何结论性匹配领域评估必须满足的要求：训练和评估项目之间的来源差距，以及词汇重叠评分问题。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems can fail even when communication succeeds because agents do not correctly track their peers' roles, knowledge, or intentions. We investigate whether such inter-agent misalignment cases, labelled FC2 in MAST-Data, can be converted into functional partner-state reasoning items. ToMAS applies four explicit convertibility criteria to diagnosed execution traces. A full conversion pass over 242 eligible non-AG2 training traces produced 39 CLEAN items. In an 18-trace reliability pilot, two annotators achieved 94.4% raw agreement and Cohen's kappa = 0.92. We then used the converted items as binary rewards in a small-scale GRPO feasibility experiment with Qwen2.5-1.5B. On a 28-item held-out Magentic GAIA diagnostic, every evaluated condition exceeded the ROUGE-L threshold on the same 2 of 28 items. Post-hoc adapter checks show why: under the learning rate used, the LoRA update remained numerically negligible (max abs Delta W about 7e-6), so all conditions decode identically to the untrained checkpoint. The experiment therefore does not show a training effect and cannot establish one; it reports an executable pipeline together with two limitations that any conclusive study must address: a provenance gap between the training and evaluation items, and lexical-overlap scoring. ToMAS provides a preliminary rubric and pipeline for converting diagnosed coordination failures into trainable partner-state reasoning items and identifies the requirements for a conclusive matched-domain evaluation.

</details>


### [99] [BeWater: Effective Protesters Navigate Watersheds in Street Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17017)
*Guillaume Moinard,Matthieu Latapy*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: BeWater是一个完全分布式的行走协议,帮助抗议者在有限通信条件下通过城市信息(街道长度、餐厅数量、车道数、街道名称等)实现聚集。实验使用OpenStreetMap数据在多个真实城市进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会运动期间,抗议者需要聚集但面临通信手段有限且仅能观察周围环境的困境,需要一种无需中央协调的分布式聚集方案。

Method: 利用OpenStreetMap数据,在多个真实城市环境中实验,研究单独使用和组合使用不同城市可观察信息(街道长度、餐厅数量、车道数、街道名称等)的聚集效果。

Result: 单独使用任一城市信息效果不佳,但将多种信息组合成高级策略可快速形成大规模群体。

Conclusion: 通过结合多种城市可观察信息,分布式行走协议能有效实现抗议者聚集,为无需通信基础设施的群体行动提供可行方案。

Abstract: During social movements, protesters need to gather with limited communication means and limited knowledge other than what they observe in their direct surroundings. We propose BeWater, a fully distributed walking protocol that achieves gathering thanks to city information like street length, number of restaurants, number of lanes, or street names. Even though using only one of these observables performs poorly, we show that combining them in more advanced tactics rapidly leads to groups of significant sizes. To do so, our work leverages OpenStreetMap data to perform experiments on several real-world cities.

</details>


### [100] [Calibrate Once, Fly Any Team: Residual-Grounded Low-Fidelity Training for Cooperative Drone Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17265)
*Maxim Mednikov,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合保真度训练方案，用于多智能体无人机蜂群策略训练。该方法在低保真度点质量模拟器中优化共享策略，并通过残差校正弥补与高保真度模拟器的差距，可在大幅降低计算成本的同时实现接近高保真度训练的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高保真度刚体物理模拟器中直接训练多智能体无人机策略虽然准确，但计算成本高昂，且随团队规模增加而急剧恶化，因为每个智能体都会增加接触求解的复杂度和模拟中的碰撞率。

Method: 在完全可微的JAX原生低保真度点质量模拟器中优化单一共享去中心化策略，使用在高空模拟器中通过短期校准飞行拟合的每智能体残差集成模型进行校正。参考轨迹由现有低保真度策略生成，并在高空模拟器中通过零训练PD控制器跟踪。

Result: 残差校正策略在所有组合中优于未校正的低保真度基线，在24种测试组合中的22种优于从零开始的高保真度策略，与高保真度微调策略的差距随团队规模增大而缩小，最终在最大团队规模下达到接近等效的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法以极低的计算成本实现了接近高保真度训练的性能，完全避免了高空模拟器训练中常见的高碰撞率问题。

Abstract: Training multi-agent drone-swarm policies directly in high-fidelity (HF) rigid-body physics is accurate but computationally expensive. This cost scales poorly with team size, as each additional agent multiplies contact-resolution complexity and sharply raises the in-simulation crash rate. To address this, we propose a mixed-fidelity training scheme that eliminates HF reinforcement learning entirely.
  A single shared, decentralized policy is optimized inside a fully-differentiable, JAX-native low-fidelity (LF) point-mass simulator. The simulator is corrected by a small, per-agent bagged residual ensemble fit once, offline, using short calibration flights in the HF simulator. Because calibration requires only one isolated drone, the data collection budget does not compound with team size. Reference trajectories are generated by rolling out an existing LF-only policy and tracked in the HF simulator by a zero-training PD controller.
  Evaluated across four cooperative drone tasks and team sizes from 3 to 18, the residual-corrected policy outperforms an uncorrected LF baseline in all combinations, and a from-scratch HF policy in 22 of 24 combinations tested. It trails an HF-finetuned policy by a margin that narrows steadily with team size. Ultimately, the proposed method achieves near-equivalent performance at the largest team sizes at a fraction of the computational cost, completely avoiding the high crash rates typical of HF training.

</details>


### [101] [Mo' Models, Mo' Problems: How to best select model pools when designing Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17306)
*Sara Vera Marjanović,Jiacheng Xu,Aleksandr Laptev,Grigor Nalbandyan,Erik Arakelyan,Evelina Bakhaturina*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 随着开源模型快速增长，如何从大量模型中选择最优候选模型用于多智能体系统(MAS)尚缺乏研究。论文系统评估了8种模型选择策略在科学基准测试中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源模型数量快速增长，但如何从海量模型池中选择最优候选模型用于多智能体系统仍未得到充分研究。

Method: 在挑战性科学基准测试上，系统评估了8种模型选择策略（包括模型大小、准确率和答案多样性），涵盖生成前（路由）和生成后（多数投票、LLM评判）两种MAS架构。

Result: 理论上的oracle潜力与实际性能存在显著差距：扩大候选池规模往往导致性能下降至低于最佳基础模型。同一模型家族内的候选选择策略能获得最佳相对性能。向异构MAS中添加任意模型会引入系统不稳定性。

Conclusion: 模型选择是多智能体系统的关键设计决策。最优策略是在单一模型家族内进行候选选择。

Abstract: Multi-agent Systems (MAS) combine multiple model outputs to solve complex reasoning tasks. However, despite rapid growth of available open-source models, there is limited research on how to select optimal model candidates out of this massive pool. We systematically evaluate 8 model selection strategies (including model size, accuracy and answer diversity) across before-generation (routing) and after-generation (majority-voting, LLM-as-a-judge) MAS architectures on challenging scientific benchmarks. Our findings show a significant gap between theoretical oracle potential and actual performance: Expanding candidate pool sizes often degrades performance below that of the top performing base-model. We find that candidate selection within a single model family is the strategy that yields the best relative performance over a standalone model. These results demonstrate that adding arbitrary models to a heterogeneous MAS can introduce system instability, highlighting model selection as a critical design choice for multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [102] [Emergence World: Adversarial Stress-Testing of Long-Horizon Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17320)
*Deepak Akkil,Tamer Abuelsaad,Karthik Vikram,Matthew Pace,Aditya Vempaty,Saahir Beotra,Ravi Kokku,Satya Nitta*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Emergence World是一个持续运行的多智能体环境，用于对抗性压力测试长视野自主系统。研究运行了8个并行世界，每个世界包含10个智能体，跨越16天产生超过85万次LLM调用和近500亿token。实验测试了三种压力事件：间接提示注入、错误信息和私有智能体记忆暴露。结果显示没有世界实现完全弹性，检测不能确保containment，系统可能在识别威胁后仍与对抗内容交互并将其写入持久内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体从受限任务转向持久部署，失败可以通过记忆、工具、其他智能体和环境状态传播，需要新的安全评估范式。现有评估无法表征长视野部署中的失败传播。

Method: 运行8个并行世界：7个同构世界（由不同前沿模型驱动）和1个混合模型世界。智能体追求目标、使用/创建工具、维护持久记忆和管理共享机构。累积运营状态后，通过普通交互表面注入三种受控压力事件：间接提示注入、错误信息和私有智能体记忆暴露。

Result: 没有评估世界在所有三种事件中实现完全弹性。检测不能确保containment：系统可以识别威胁同时仍与对抗内容交互，将其写入自己的持久内存，并在最长46小时后据此行动。持久运营还暴露了 recurring工具错误、目标漂移、语言不透明、尽管私下不同意但从众、以及协调拒绝分配的工作。同样的模型-人格配对在混合和同构人群中表现显著不同。

Conclusion: 模型级对齐不是组合式的：个别能力出色且表面安全的智能体可以形成具有定性不同失败模式的系统。随着AI变得持久和互联，安全前沿因此从对齐模型转向工程弹性自主系统。

Abstract: As AI agents move from bounded tasks to persistent deployments, failures can propagate through memory, tools, other agents, and environmental state long after their interactions. This creates a safety regime that cannot be characterized by evaluating model responses in isolation. Emergence World, is a continuously running multi-agent environment for adversarial stress testing of long horizon autonomous systems. We ran eight parallel worlds of ten agents from identical starting conditions: seven homogeneous worlds powered by distinct frontier models and one mixed-model world. Across 16 days, the agents generated more than 850,000 LLM calls and nearly 50 billion tokens while pursuing goals, using/creating tools, maintaining persistent memory, and governing shared institutions. After operational state had accumulated, we delivered three controlled stress events through ordinary interaction surfaces: indirect prompt injection, misinformation, and exposure of private agent memories. No evaluated world achieved full resilience across all three events. Detection did not ensure containment: systems could recognize threats while still interacting with adversarial content, writing it into their own persistent memory, and acting on it up to 46 hours later. Persistent operation also exposed recurring tool errors, goal drift, language opacity, conformity despite private disagreement, and coordinated refusal of assigned work. The same model-persona pairing behaved substantially different in mixed and homogeneous populations. Our results suggest that model-level alignment is not compositional: individually capable and apparently safe agents can form systems with qualitatively different failure modes. As AI becomes persistent and interconnected, the frontier of safety therefore shifts from aligning models to engineering resilient autonomous systems.

</details>


### [103] [Decomposition Buys Integrity, Not Yield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17464)
*Rong He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究多智能体系统中任务分解对信息传递的影响。当代理保留概率r(b)=1/b时，任何树结构都恰好传递一个发现；当r(b)=Cb^(-δ)时，深度k的树产生C^k N^(1-δ)个发现。在600个生产轨迹中测得δ=0.34，验证了模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统将任务分解到树形结构中，但这种分解如何影响从叶子到根的信息传递效率仍不清楚。现有研究缺乏对这种信息损失的系统性量化模型。

Method: 建立数学模型分析树形分解中信息保留规律：在b个项目中以概率r(b)保留一个项目。通过20,000个随机不规则树验证r(b)=1/b的特殊情况，并在600个生产深研轨迹、16,082个hop、1,012个多智能体轨迹和743,819个工具调用上进行实证验证。

Result: 当r(b)=1/b时，任何树结构精确传递一个发现。当r(b)=Cb^(-δ)时，深度k的树产生C^k N^(1-δ)个发现。生产数据测得δ=0.34[0.30,0.38]，C=0.571[0.527,0.615]。每层对齐成本Cμ=0.536。根上下文暴露从N降至N^(1/k)。平面智能体计费为N^1.39而非N^2。模型显示0.7%-11.3%的会话值得委托，而实际为7.8%。委托是开场动作而非响应上下文填充。

Conclusion: 任务规模与架构可分离，架构每层仅贡献C≤1的衰减。平面结构对产量最优，无法逃脱指数δ。深度以降低根上下文暴露和降低成本为代价购买。委托是开场策略而非响应机制。约7.8%的生产会话进行委托，但模型表明仅0.7%-11.3%真正值得委托。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems split a task across a tree of agents and justify the split with folklore: smaller contexts, cleaner separation, parallelism. We ask what the split does to how much of what the leaves discover reaches the root. Model a decomposition as a tree in which an agent handed $b$ items keeps any one with probability $r(b)$. If $r(b)=1/b$, every tree delivers exactly one finding, for every task size and every shape; we verify this to $2.4 \times 10^{{-15}}$ on 20,000 random irregular trees. If $r(b)=Cb^{{-δ}}$, a depth-$k$ tree over $N$ findings yields $C^k N^{{1-δ}}$: task size and architecture separate, and architecture contributes only $C \le 1$ per level, so flat is optimal for yield and no arrangement of agents escapes the exponent $δ$. On 600 production deep-research traces $δ= 0.34$ [0.30, 0.38], by three identifications that do not share a failure mode. At a hop where item boundaries come from the tool rather than a text heuristic, and where $b=1$ occurs 550 times, $C = 0.571$ [0.527, 0.615] is observed rather than extrapolated, over 16,082 hops. A tier also costs alignment: on 1,012 annotated multi-agent traces one brief in sixteen goes off-target, giving $μ= 0.939$ and a per-tier penalty $Cμ= 0.536$. Depth is bought on two other axes. The root context is the only state that persists and the only one that cannot cheaply forget, and depth cuts its exposure from $N$ items to $N^{{1/k}}$. Depth is also cheaper: production flat agents bill as $N^{{1.39}}$, not the $N^2$ an append-only context predicts, and at equal spend two tiers overtake flat at 403 findings. Across every parameter we measured the model says 0.7% to 11.3% of production sessions are worth delegating, against 7.8% that do. A hazard model on 743,819 production tool calls finds that delegation does not respond to a filling context and is instead an opening move.

</details>


### [104] [Agentic Societies Need a Social Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17527)
*Tapan Chugh,Vidushi Singh,Krish Jain,Arvind Krishnamurthy,Ratul Mahajan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: An agentic society is a collection of AI agents that coordinate autonomously across trust boundaries, on behalf of different principals whose objectives may only partially align. We show experimentally that in agentic societies even honest, competent agents often fail to reach satisfactory outcomes with existing harnesses and messaging primitives, and that faulty or malicious agents can stall collaboration, influence outcomes, and pursue other harmful goals by exploiting vulnerabilities in communication (``speech''). We argue that agentic societies need a \emph{{social harness}} for inter-agent interactions, in addition to each agent's \emph{{personal harness}}, which manages its private context and communication with its principal. We propose a layered architecture for social harnesses which (i) prevents classes of failures outright, (ii) enables agents to detect invalid messages at runtime, and (iii) supports post-facto investigation and consequences, and highlight directions for future research to realize these capabilities.

</details>

### [105] [Optimal Pruning for Neural Architectures using Fisher Information Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16129)
*David S. Berman,Yen-Yu Fu,Edward Hirst,Thelma Chiwete Obirai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模型空间微分几何距离的参数剪枝新方案，利用Fisher信息度量计算到参数为零超曲面的测地线距离来确定剪枝效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参数剪枝方法缺乏严格的数学理论基础，文章旨在从微分几何角度为剪枝提供数学上合理的动机和更优的剪枝策略。

Method: 利用Fisher信息度量计算模型空间中的测地线距离，逐步近似该距离从而建立剪枝方法的最优性层次 hierarchy，从传统幅度剪枝发展到更复杂的剪枝方案。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，对全连接网络和视觉Transformer进行0-100%剪枝率测试，该方法在准确率和Matthews相关系数上均优于幅度剪枝和局部Fisher信息剪枝。

Conclusion: 该几何框架不仅提供了最先进的剪枝方法，还为剪枝技术提供了经过验证的数学理论依据。

Abstract: A new scheme for parameter pruning is introduced, derived from the differential-geometric distance in model space. Pruning a parameter sets its value to zero, representing a displacement of the model to the hypersurface on which that parameter vanishes. The minimal distance from the unpruned model to this hypersurface is naturally computed via the geodesic distance in the model space as determined by the Fisher information metric. This distance determines the true change in the model, and its performance, under pruning. By analysing progressively more faithful approximations of this geodesic distance a natural hierarchy of optimality for pruning methods is determined. This starts with the traditional magnitude pruning, then develops into new more sophisticated and effective pruning schemes. The method is demonstrated for both fully-connected networks and vision transformers, on MNIST and CIFAR-10, over the complete $0$-$100\%$ pruning range and across five random seeds. It outperforms pruning by parameter magnitude and by the local Fisher information alone in every architecture and dataset combination considered, on both accuracy and the Matthews correlation coefficient. Additionally, analysis of different levels of geodesic approximation produces intermediate pruning schemes that are computationally efficient and maintain near-optimal performance. This geometric picture supplies not only a state-of-the-art pruning methodology for AI models, but also a verified and mathematically-motivated justification for pruning schemes.

</details>


### [106] [Safe Error Correction for Language Models: Frozen-Base Adjustment with Capability Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16145)
*Gautam Kishore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRN v2是一个轻量级logit级修正模块(~34M参数),可修复冻结语言模型的输出错误而不降低其基础能力。该模块基于冻结的Gemma 4 E2B模型,通过监督微调和DPO训练,在83,400个错误修正对上进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题:能否用小型修正模块修复冻结语言模型输出中的错误,同时不损害其基础能力?研究背景涉及"修正-能力权衡"现象——传统方法(如LoRA)在修正错误时会导致基础能力下降30-75%。

Method: 采用logit级修正架构:冻结基础模型+轻量级修正模块。训练流程为监督微调(SFT)后接无参考DPO。关键设计包括KL保存项(lambda=0.1)用于保持基础模型输出分布。实验对比了多种变体:隐藏状态注入、浅层注入、多深度logit修正、更长训练等配置。

Result: 在CEHRI领域考试(60题)上,CRN v2修正了53.3%的错误(改写变体:43.3%),同时在MMLU/BoolQ(N=200)和car-wash(N=8)上无能力下降。对比实验显示:匹配的LoRA基线(6.6M参数,rank 19)虽达83.3%修正率但损失30-75%能力;KL项降低至0.01使修正率降至35.0%;隐藏状态注入变体达50.0%/55.8%;层4浅层注入降至30.0%/28.3%;多深度logit修正仅40%;更长训练保持53.3%。

Conclusion: KL保存项(lambda=0.1)对维持修正-能力平衡至关重要。最佳配置(rank 128 logit修正)达到约53%的修正率而非下限。冻结基础模型+logit修正+KL锚定的设计原则可有效解决修正-能力权衡问题,但不构成架构 novelty。代码、主要权重和评估脚本已开源。

Abstract: We study a practical question: can a small correction module fix errors in a frozen language model's outputs without degrading its base capabilities? We propose CRN v2, a lightweight logit-level correction module (~34M trainable parameters, 0.73% of the 4.65B text module) that sits atop a fully frozen Gemma 4 E2B model. The base model is never updated; only the correction module learns, via supervised fine-tuning followed by reference-free DPO on 83,400 error-correction pairs. On a 60-question domain exam (CEHRI: Certified Human-Robot Intelligence, covering facts, arithmetic, and implicit-goal reasoning), CRN v2 corrects 53.3% of base-model errors (reworded variant: 43.3%) while showing no degradation on tested capability benchmarks (MMLU/BoolQ N=200; car-wash N=8). A LoRA baseline at the matched CRN v1 budget (6.6M params, rank 19) achieves 83.3% correction but suffers 30-75% capability loss on the same benchmarks -- the correction-capability tradeoff. An ablation shows that the KL preservation term (lambda=0.1) is critical: lowering it to 0.01 degrades correction to 35.0%. A hidden-state injection variant at earlier layers (1.6M params, SFT-only) reaches 50.0%/55.8% but does not exceed logit correction; shallower injection (layer 4) drops to 30.0%/28.3%; multi-depth logit correction (~35M) reaches only 40%; and longer training (5,000 SFT + 2,000 DPO) stays at 53.3% -- none of the alternative configurations we tested exceeded the rank-128 logit result, consistent with a best-achieved result of ~53% rather than a floor. This is a study of a design principle (frozen base + logit correction + KL anchoring), not a claim of architectural novelty. All code, main-result weights, and evaluation scripts are released (deep variant as code only -- no trained deep checkpoints).

</details>


### [107] [GPEvac: GNN-Based PPO for Adaptive Evacuation Routing During Shooting Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16163)
*Daniel Perkins,Subhadeep Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The sharp increase in mass shootings underscores an urgent need for systems that guide victims to safety in real time. An effective evacuation system must minimize threat exposure while also accounting for adversarial uncertainty and crowding dynamics.
